        "plan": "### 1. 代码整体结构\n\n本项目采用微服务架构，文件结构按服务边界划分，根目录下包含 `backend` (Python), `middleware` (Node.js), `vscode-ext` (TypeScript), `infra` (Docker) 四个核心目录。\n\n#### 1.1 Python 智能后端 (`backend/`)\n- **`app/main.py`**: FastAPI 应用入口，定义路由挂载、生命周期事件（启动/关闭）及全局异常处理。\n- **`app/api/routes.py`**: 定义 `/chat/stream` 和 `/health` 接口，处理请求验证与响应流构建。\n- **`app/services/qwen_client.py`**: 封装 Qwen API 调用逻辑，包含异步请求发送、流式解析、重试机制及超时控制。\n- **`app/services/context_manager.py`**: 管理会话上下文，实现滑动窗口算法以限制 Token 数量，防止内存溢出。\n- **`app/core/config.py`**: 基于 Pydantic 的环境变量加载器，校验 `QWEN_API_KEY`, `REDIS_URL` 等关键配置。\n- **`app/models/schemas.py`**: 定义 Pydantic 模型，规范请求体（Prompt, SessionID）与响应体（Chunk, ErrorInfo）。\n\n#### 1.2 Node.js 通信中间件 (`middleware/`)\n- **`src/index.ts`**: 服务入口，初始化 HTTP 服务器与 WebSocket 监听器。\n- **`src/proxy/ws-handler.ts`**: 处理 VS Code 插件的 WebSocket 连接，维护长连接心跳，转发消息至 Python 后端。\n- **`src/proxy/load-balancer.ts`**: 实现简单的轮询或最少连接数策略，将请求分发至多个 Python 后端实例（预留扩展性）。\n- **`src/utils/retry-strategy.ts`**: 实现指数退避重试逻辑，处理网络波动导致的临时断连。\n- **`src/types/interfaces.ts`**: 定义内部通信协议类型，确保前后端数据结构一致。\n\n#### 1.3 VS Code 前端插件 (`vscode-ext/`)\n- **`src/extension.ts`**: 插件激活入口，注册命令与侧边栏视图。\n- **`src/client/socket-client.ts`**: 封装 WebSocket 客户端，负责连接中间件、发送指令及接收流式数据。\n- **`src/ui/chat-panel.ts`**: 实现 Webview 面板，渲染聊天界面，处理用户输入与流式文本的动态追加。\n- **`src/utils/editor-context.ts`**: 捕获当前编辑器选中代码或活动文件内容，格式化后作为上下文发送。\n\n#### 1.4 基础设施与配置 (`infra/`)\n- **`docker-compose.yml`**: 编排 `backend`, `middleware`, `redis` 三个服务，定义网络、端口映射及依赖关系。\n- **`backend/Dockerfile`**: 多阶段构建 Python 镜像，基于 Alpine，安装依赖并配置非 root 用户运行。\n- **`middleware/Dockerfile`**: 多阶段构建 Node.js 镜像，优化层缓存，生产环境仅保留必要文件。\n- **`.env.example`**: 环境变量模板，包含占位符供部署时填充。\n\n---\n\n### 2. 每个函数的职责\n\n#### Python Backend\n- **`stream_chat_response(prompt: str, session_id: str) -> AsyncGenerator`**: \n  - 职责：协调上下文管理器获取历史，调用 Qwen 客户端发起流式请求，逐块 yield 响应数据，并在发生错误时 yield 错误标记。\n- **`manage_context(session_id: str, new_msg: dict) -> List[dict]`**: \n  - 职责：从 Redis 读取历史，追加新消息，若总 Token 数超过阈值则移除最早的消息，最后将更新后的列表写回 Redis。\n- **`call_qwen_api(messages: List[dict]) -> AsyncIterator[str]`**: \n  - 职责：构造 HTTP 请求头，发送 POST 请求至 Qwen  endpoint，解析 SSE (Server-Sent Events) 流，提取 content 字段并抛出网络异常。\n\n#### Node.js Middleware\n- **`handleWebSocketConnection(ws: WebSocket) -> void`**: \n  - 职责：建立连接时生成唯一连接 ID，注册消息监听器；收到消息时透传至 Python 后端；收到后端响应时转发给 VS Code 插件。\n- **`heartbeatMonitor() -> void`**: \n  - 职责：定时向所有活跃连接发送 Ping 包，若 N 次未收到 Pong 则主动断开连接并清理资源。\n- **`forwardRequest(payload: any) -> Promise<any>`**: \n  - 职责：根据负载均衡策略选择后端实例地址，发起 HTTP/WebSocket 请求，处理超时与重试逻辑。\n\n#### VS Code Extension\n- **`sendCommandToMiddleware(command: string, context: string) -> void`**: \n  - 职责：序列化用户指令与编辑器上下文，通过 WebSocket 发送至中间件，并触发 UI 更新以显示“思考中”状态。\n- **`renderStreamChunk(chunk: string) -> void`**: \n  - 职责：在 Webview 中安全地追加文本内容，处理 Markdown 渲染逻辑，保持滚动条在底部。\n- **`captureActiveEditorContext() -> string | null`**: \n  - 职责：检查是否有活动编辑器，若有则获取选中文本或全文，进行脱敏或截断处理后返回。\n\n---\n\n### 3. 输入与输出设计\n\n#### 接口契约 (API Contract)\n- **请求格式 (VS Code -> Middleware -> Backend)**:\n  ```json\n  {\n    \"session_id\": \"uuid-v4-string\",\n    \"prompt\": \"用户自然语言指令\",\n    \"context_code\": \"可选：选中的代码片段\",\n    \"history_summary\": \"可选：前序对话摘要\"\n  }\n  ```\n- **响应格式 (Backend -> Middleware -> VS Code)**:\n  - 流式数据块 (SSE):\n    ```text\n    data: {\"type\": \"chunk\", \"content\": \"生成的代码片段...\"}\n    data: {\"type\": \"chunk\", \"content\": \"解释说明...\"}\n    data: {\"type\": \"end\", \"usage\": {\"total_tokens\": 150}}\n    ```\n  - 错误响应:\n    ```json\n    {\n      \"type\": \"error\",\n      \"code\": \"RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED\",\n      \"message\": \"Qwen API 限流，请稍后重试\"\n    }\n    ```\n\n#### 数据存储设计 (Redis)\n- **Key**: `session:{session_id}:history`\n- **Value**: JSON 数组 `[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"...\"}, {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"...\"}]`\n- **TTL**: 30 分钟 (无操作自动过期)\n\n---\n\n### 4. 伪代码\n\n#### Python 上下文滑动窗口逻辑\n```python\nFUNCTION manage_context(session_id, new_message):\n    history = redis.get(f\"session:{session_id}:history\") OR []\n    history.append(new_message)\n    \n    current_tokens = calculate_tokens(history)\n    MAX_TOKENS = 4096\n    \n    WHILE current_tokens > MAX_TOKENS AND length(history) > 1:\n        # 移除最旧的对话轮次 (保留系统提示词)\n        removed_msg = history.pop(1) \n        current_tokens -= calculate_tokens([removed_msg])\n    \n    redis.setex(f\"session:{session_id}:history\", 1800, serialize(history))\n    RETURN history\n```\n\n#### Node.js 心跳检测逻辑\n```typescript\nFUNCTION startHeartbeat(connection):\n    interval = SET_INTERVAL every 30 seconds:\n        IF connection.readyState is OPEN:\n            SEND ping message\n            INCREMENT fail_count\n            IF fail_count > 3:\n                CLOSE connection with reason \"Timeout\"\n        ELSE:\n            CLEAR_INTERVAL\n            CLEANUP resources\n            \n    ON receive pong:\n        RESET fail_count to 0\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. 需要注意的边界情况\n\n1.  **API 限流与降级**: 当 Qwen API 返回 429 状态码时，后端不应直接崩溃，而应捕获异常，向客户端返回友好的“服务繁忙”提示，并在日志中记录以便后续分析。需实现指数退避重试（如 1s, 2s, 4s），最多重试 3 次。\n2.  **网络断连重连**: 免费云资源网络不稳定。Node.js 中间件需具备自动重连 Python 后端的能力；VS Code 插件需在 WebSocket 断开时提示用户“正在重连...\"，并在重连成功后自动恢复会话状态（通过 SessionID）。\n3.  **内存溢出保护**: 必须严格实施 Token 计数和滑动窗口机制。若单条用户输入过长（超过模型上限），需在发送前进行截断并提示用户“输入过长，已自动截断”。\n4.  **并发会话隔离**: Redis Key 必须严格绑定 `session_id`，防止多用户或多标签页之间的上下文混淆。在分布式部署下，需确保 Redis 连接的线程安全性。\n5.  **冷启动延迟**: 针对免费云资源的休眠机制，Node.js 中间件可配置一个轻量级的“保活”定时任务（如每 14 分钟访问一次自身健康检查接口），或由 VS Code 插件在用户打开插件时发送预热请求。\n6.  **敏感信息泄露**: 严禁在日志中打印完整的 `API_KEY` 或用户代码中的敏感硬编码密钥。所有日志输出需经过脱敏处理。\n\n---\n\nTEST_COMMAND: pytest backend/tests && npm test --prefix middleware && vscode-test",

number = 3,\n  baseDelay: number = 1000\n): Promise<T> {\n  let lastError: Error | null = null;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i <= maxRetries; i++) {\n    try {\n      return await operation();\n    } catch (error) {\n      lastError = error as Error;\n      if (i === maxRetries) break;\n      \n      const delay = baseDelay * Math.pow(2, i);\n      console.log(`Retry attempt ${i + 1} in ${delay}ms...`);\n      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));\n    }\n  }\n\n  throw lastError || new Error('Operation failed after retries');\n}\n\n# FILE: (msg) => {\n        if (msg.type === 'chunk' && msg.content) {\n          ChatPanel.currentPanel?.appendChunk(msg.content);\n        } else if (msg.type === 'end') {\n Documentation\n\n## Overview\nAlphaPilot is a distributed AI coding assistant designed for VS Code, utilizing a microservices architecture to ensure scalability and deployability on free cloud tiers.\n\n## Components\n\n### 1. Backend (Python/FastAPI)\n- **Role**: Core intelligence. Handles LLM interaction (Qwen API) and state management.\n- **Tech Stack**: FastAPI, Uvicorn, Redis, HTTPX.\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Asynchronous streaming responses (SSE).\n  - Context window management using sliding window algorithm in Redis.\n  - Robust retry logic for external API calls.\n\n### 2. Middleware (Node.js)\n- **Role**: Communication bridge and load balancer.\n- **Tech Stack**: Express, WS (WebSocket), TypeScript.\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Maintains persistent WebSocket connections with VS Code clients.\n  - Routes requests to available Python backend instances.\n  - Handles connection resilience and heartbeat monitoring.\n\n### 3. VS Code Extension (TypeScript)\n- **Role**: User Interface.\n- **Tech Stack**: VS Code API, Webviews.\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Captures editor context (selected code).\n  - Renders streaming markdown responses in a dedicated panel.\n  - Manages reconnection logic transparently.\n\n### 4. Infrastructure (Docker)\n- **Role**: Orchestration.\n- **Components**:\n  - `docker-compose.yml`: Defines the service topology.\n  - `Redis`: Stateful storage for conversation history.\n\n## Data Flow\n1. User types in VS Code -> Extension captures context.\n2. Extension sends JSON via WebSocket to Middleware.\n3. Middleware forwards HTTP request to Backend.\n4. Backend retrieves history from Redis, calls Qwen API.\n5. Qwen streams tokens -> Backend yields SSE events.\n6. Middleware relays SSE events to Extension.\n7. Extension renders text in Webview.\n8. Backend updates Redis with new turn.\n\n## Deployment\nThe system is designed to be deployed on platforms like Render, Railway, or Fly.io using the provided `docker-compose.yml` (adapted for single container or separate services) and `.env` configuration.\n\n# META:\n{\"version\": \"1.0\", \"author\": \"AlphaPilot\", \"project_name\": \"AlphaPilot OS\", \"license\": \"MIT\"}\n\n# DEPENDS:\n{\"requirements\": [\"python>=3.11\", \"node>=18\", \"docker>=20.0\", \"redis>=6.0\"]}",

            "content": "import redis\nimport json\nimport tiktoken\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional\nfrom app.models.schemas import Message, Role\nfrom app.core.config import settings\n\nclass ContextManager:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)\n        try:\n            self.encoder = tiktoken.encoding_for_model(\"gpt-3.5-turbo\") # Approximation for Qwen\n        except KeyError:\n            self.encoder = tiktoken.get_encoding(\"cl100k_base\")\n\n    def _count_tokens(self, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> int:\n        num_tokens = 0\n        for message in messages:\n            num_tokens += 4  # Every message follows <im_start>{role/name}\\n{content}<im_end>\\n\n            for key, value in message.items():\n                num_tokens += len(self.encoder.encode(str(value)))\n        return num_tokens\n\n    def get_history(self, session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n        key = f\"session:{session_id}:history\"\n        data = self.redis_client.get(key)\n        if data:\n            return json.loads(data)\n        return []\n\n    def update_history(self, session_id: str, new_message: Dict[str, str]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n        key = f\"session:{session_id}:history\"\n        history = self.get_history(session_id)\n        \n        # Append new message\n        history.append(new_message)\n        \n        # Sliding window logic\n        current_tokens = self._count_tokens(history)\n        max_tokens = settings.MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS\n        \n        # Keep at least system prompt if exists (index 0), otherwise keep at least 1 message\n        min_messages = 1\n        if len(history) > 0 and history[0].get('role') == 'system':\n            min_messages = 1 # Keep system\n            \n        while current_tokens > max_tokens and len(history) > min_messages:\n            # Remove oldest non-system message\n            removed_idx = 1 if len(history) > 1 and history[0].get('role') == 'system' else 0\n            removed_msg = history.pop(removed_idx)\n            current_tokens -= self._count_tokens([removed_msg])\n            \n        # Save back to Redis with TTL (30 mins)\n        self.redis_client.setex(key, 1800, json.dumps(history))\n        return history",

            "content": "import httpx\nimport json\nimport asyncio\nfrom typing import AsyncGenerator, List, Dict, Any\nfrom app.core.config import settings\nfrom app.models.schemas import Message\n\nclass QwenClient:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.api_key = settings.QWEN_API_KEY\n        self.api_url = settings.QWEN_API_URL\n        self.model = settings.QWEN_MODEL_NAME\n        self.headers = {\n            \"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {self.api_key}\",\n            \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"\n        }\n\n    async def stream_chat(self, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:\n        payload = {\n            \"model\": self.model,\n            \"messages\": messages,\n            \"stream\": True,\n            \"temperature\": 0.7\n        }\n\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:\n            retry_count = 0\n            max_retries = 3\n            \n            while retry_count <= max_retries:\n                try:\n                    async with client.stream(\"POST\", self.api_url, json=payload, headers=self.headers) as response:\n                        if response.status_code == 429:\n                            wait_time = 2 ** retry_count\n                            await asyncio.sleep(wait_time)\n                            retry_count += 1\n                            continue\n                        \n                        response.raise_for_status()\n                        \n                        async for line in response.aiter_lines():\n                            if line.startswith(\"data: \"):\n                                data_str = line[6:]\n                                if data_str.strip() == \"[DONE]\":\n                                    break\n                                try:\n                                    data = json.loads(data_str)\n                                    if \"choices\" in data and len(data[\"choices\"]) > 0:\n                                        delta = data[\"choices\"][0].get(\"delta\", {})\n                                        content = delta.get(\"content\", \"\")\n                                        if content:\n                                            yield content\n                                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                                    continue\n                        return # Success, exit loop\n                \n                except (httpx.RequestError, httpx.HTTPStatusError) as e:\n                    if retry_count >= max_retries:\n                        raise Exception(f\"Qwen API request failed after retries: {str(e)}\")\n                    wait_time = 2 ** retry_count\n                    await asyncio.sleep(wait_time)\n                    retry_count += 1",

Here we just save the user msg for now \n        # and assume the client will send the full turn later or we buffer internally.\n        # Simplified: Save user msg now. Assistant msg saving requires buffering the whole stream first.\n        # To keep streaming simple, we will buffer the response in memory for this implementation to save to Redis.\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        error_resp = {\"type\": \"error\", \"code\": \"GENERATION_FAILED\", \"message\": str(e), \"session_id\": session_id}\n        yield f\"data: {json.dumps(error_resp)}\\n\\n\"\n        return\n\n    # Note: In a real production scenario, we would buffer the stream to save the full assistant response to Redis.\n    # For this micro-architecture demo, we save the user message immediately.\n    context_manager.update_history(session_id, user_msg)\n    \n    # Simulate saving assistant response (in real impl, buffer the chunks above)\n    # For now, we just signal end.\n    yield f\"data: {json.dumps({'type': 'end', 'usage': {'total_tokens': 0}, 'session_id': session_id})}\\n\\n\"\n\n@router.post(\"/chat/stream\")\nasync def chat_stream(request: ChatRequest):\n    return StreamingResponse(generate_stream_response(request), media_type=\"text/event-stream\")\n\n@router.get(\"/health\")\nasync def health_check():\n    return {\"status\": \"healthy\", \"service\": \"alphapilot-backend\"}",

            "content": "export async function retryWithBackoff<T>(\n  operation: () => Promise<T>,\n  maxRetries: number = 3,\n  baseDelay: number = 1000\n): Promise<T> {\n  let lastError: Error | null = null;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i <= maxRetries; i++) {\n    try {\n      return await operation();\n    } catch (error) {\n      lastError = error as Error;\n      if (i === maxRetries) break;\n      \n      const delay = baseDelay * Math.pow(2, i);\n      console.log(`Retry attempt ${i + 1} in ${delay}ms...`);\n      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));\n    }\n  }\n\n  throw lastError || new Error('Operation failed after retries');\n}",

            "content": "import WebSocket from 'ws';\nimport { forwardRequestToBackend } from './load-balancer';\nimport { ChatRequest, ChatMessage } from '../types/interfaces';\n\nexport function handleWebSocketConnection(ws: WebSocket) {\n  console.log('New client connected');\n\n  ws.on('message', async (message: string) => {\n    try {\n      const request: ChatRequest = JSON.parse(message);\n      console.log(`Received request for session: ${request.session_id}`);\n\n      const backendResponse = await forwardRequestToBackend(request);\n      \n      if (!backendResponse.body) {\n        throw new Error('No response body from backend');\n      }\n\n      const reader = backendResponse.body.getReader();\n      const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n\n      while (true) {\n        const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n        if (done) break;\n\n        const chunk = decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n        // Forward SSE data directly to client\n        ws.send(chunk);\n      }\n\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Error processing message:', error);\n      const errorMsg: ChatMessage = {\n        type: 'error',\n        code: 'PROXY_ERROR',\n        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error',\n        session_id: (JSON.parse(message) as ChatRequest).session_id\n      };\n      ws.send(JSON.stringify(errorMsg));\n    }\n  });\n\n  ws.on('close', () => {\n    console.log('Client disconnected');\n  });\n\n  ws.on('error', (err) => {\n    console.error('WebSocket error:', err);\n  });\n}",

reconnectTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;\n  private readonly url: string;\n\n  constructor(middlewareUrl: string) {\n    this.url = middlewareUrl.replace('http', 'ws');\n  }\n\n  connect(onMessage: (msg: ChatMessage) => void, onError: (err: any) => void) {\n    if (this.socket && this.socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      this.socket = new WebSocket(this.url);\n\n      this.socket.onopen = () => {\n        console.log('Connected to AlphaPilot Middleware');\n        if (this.reconnectTimer) {\n          clearTimeout(this.reconnectTimer);\n          this.reconnectTimer = null;\n        }\n      };\n\n      this.socket.onmessage = (event) => {\n        const data = event.data;\n        // Handle SSE-like format or raw JSON\n        if (typeof data === 'string' && data.startsWith('data: ')) {\n          try {\n            const jsonStr = data.substring(6);\n            const msg: ChatMessage = JSON.parse(jsonStr);\n            onMessage(msg);\n          } catch (e) {\n            console.error('Failed to parse message', e);\n          }\n        } else {\n          try {\n            const msg: ChatMessage = JSON.parse(data);\n            onMessage(msg);\n          } catch (e) {\n            console.error('Failed to parse raw JSON', e);\n          }\n        }\n      };\n\n      this.socket.onerror = (err) => {\n        console.error('Socket error', err);\n        onError(err);\n      };\n\n      this.socket.onclose = () => {\n        console.log('Socket closed, attempting reconnect...');\n        this.scheduleReconnect(onMessage, onError);\n      };\n\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Connection failed', error);\n      this.scheduleReconnect(onMessage, onError);\n    }\n  }\n\n  send(payload: any) {\n    if (this.socket && this.socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {\n      this.socket.send(JSON.stringify(payload));\n    } else {\n      vscode.window.showErrorMessage('Not connected to AlphaPilot service.');\n    }\n  }\n\n  private scheduleReconnect(onMessage: any, onError: any) {\n    if (this.reconnectTimer) return;\n    this.reconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      this.connect(onMessage, onError);\n    }, 3000);\n  }\n\n  disconnect() {\n    if (this.socket) {\n      this.socket.close();\n      this.socket = null;\n    }\n    if (this.reconnectTimer) {\n      clearTimeout(this.reconnectTimer);\n      this.reconnectTimer = null;\n    }\n  }\n}",

          {
            "op": "create",
            "path": "docs/architecture.md",
            "content": "# AlphaPilot OS Architecture Documentation\n\n## Overview\nAlphaPilot is a distributed AI coding assistant designed for VS Code, utilizing a microservices architecture to ensure scalability and deployability on free cloud tiers.\n\n## Components\n\n### 1. Backend (Python/FastAPI)\n- **Role**: Core intelligence. Handles LLM interaction (Qwen API) and state management.\n- **Tech Stack**: FastAPI, Uvicorn, Redis, HTTPX.\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Asynchronous streaming responses (SSE).\n  - Context window management using sliding window algorithm in Redis.\n  - Robust retry logic for external API calls.\n\n### 2. Middleware (Node.js)\n- **Role**: Communication bridge and load balancer.\n- **Tech Stack**: Express, WS (WebSocket), TypeScript.\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Maintains persistent WebSocket connections with VS Code clients.\n  - Routes requests to available Python backend instances.\n  - Handles connection resilience and heartbeat monitoring.\n\n### 3. VS Code Extension (TypeScript)\n- **Role**: User Interface.\n- **Tech Stack**: VS Code API, Webviews.\n- **Key Features**:\n  - Captures editor context (selected code).\n  - Renders streaming markdown responses in a dedicated panel.\n  - Manages reconnection logic transparently.\n\n### 4. Infrastructure (Docker)\n- **Role**: Orchestration.\n- **Components**:\n  - `docker-compose.yml`: Defines the service topology.\n  - `Redis`: Stateful storage for conversation history.\n\n## Data Flow\n1. User types in VS Code -> Extension captures context.\n2. Extension sends JSON via WebSocket to Middleware.\n3. Middleware forwards HTTP request to Backend.\n4. Backend retrieves history from Redis, calls Qwen API.\n5. Qwen streams tokens -> Backend yields SSE events.\n6. Middleware relays SSE events to Extension.\n7. Extension renders text in Webview.\n8. Backend updates Redis with new turn.\n\n## Deployment\nThe system is designed to be deployed on platforms like Render, Railway, or Fly.io using the provided `docker-compose.yml` (adapted for single container or separate services) and `.env` configuration.",

        "content": "import redis\nimport json\nimport tiktoken\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional\nfrom app.models.schemas import Message, Role\nfrom app.core.config import settings\n\nclass ContextManager:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)\n        try:\n            # Use cl100k_base which covers gpt-3.5/4 and is a good approximation for Qwen\n            self.encoder = tiktoken.get_encoding(\"cl100k_base\")\n        except KeyError:\n            self.encoder = tiktoken.get_encoding(\"cl100k_base\")\n\n    def _count_message_tokens(self, message: Dict[str, str]) -> int:\n        \"\"\"\n        Counts tokens for a single message dict {'role': '...', 'content': '...'}.\n        Approximation based on OpenAI format: 4 tokens per message + tokens for content.\n        \"\"\"\n        num_tokens = 4  # Base cost per message\n        for key, value in message.items():\n            num_tokens += len(self.encoder.encode(str(value)))\n        return num_tokens\n\n    def get_history(self, session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n        key = f\"session:{session_id}:history\"\n        data = self.redis_client.get(key)\n        if data:\n            try:\n                return json.loads(data)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                return []\n        return []\n\n    def update_history(self, session_id: str, new_message: Dict[str, str]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n        key = f\"session:{session_id}:history\"\n        history = self.get_history(session_id)\n        \n        # Append new message\n        history.append(new_message)\n        \n        # Calculate initial total tokens\n        current_tokens = sum(self._count_message_tokens(msg) for msg in history)\n        max_tokens = settings.MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS\n        \n        # Determine minimum messages to keep (preserve system prompt if present)\n        min_messages = 0\n        if len(history) > 0 and history[0].get('role') == 'system':\n            min_messages = 1\n            \n        # Sliding window: Remove oldest non-system messages until under limit\n        while current_tokens > max_tokens and len(history) > min_messages:\n            # Identify index to remove (skip index 0 if it's system)\n            remove_idx = 0\n            if len(history) > 1 and history[0].get('role') == 'system':\n                remove_idx = 1\n            \n            removed_msg = history.pop(remove_idx)\n            # Decrement token count by the removed message's cost\n            current_tokens -= self._count_message_tokens(removed_msg)\n            \n        # Save back to Redis with TTL (30 mins)\n        self.redis_client.setex(key, 1800, json.dumps(history))\n        return history",

Construct full assistant response\n        full_assistant_content = \"\".join(collected_chunks)\n        assistant_msg = {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": full_assistant_content}\n        \n        #         "content": "import redis\nimport json\nimport tiktoken\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional\nfrom app.models.schemas import Message, Role\nfrom app.core.config import settings\n\nclass ContextManager:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.redis_client = redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)\n        try:\n            # Use cl100k_base which covers gpt-3.5/4 and is a good approximation for Qwen\n            self.encoder = tiktoken.get_encoding(\"cl100k_base\")\n        except KeyError:\n            self.encoder = tiktoken.get_encoding(\"cl100k_base\")\n\n    def _count_message_tokens(self, message: Dict[str, str]) -> int:\n        \"\"\"\n        Counts tokens for a single message dict {'role': '...', 'content': '...'}.\n        Approximation based on OpenAI format: 4 tokens per message + tokens for content.\n        \"\"\"\n        num_tokens = 4  # Base cost per message\n        for key, value in message.items():\n            num_tokens += len(self.encoder.encode(str(value)))\n        return num_tokens\n\n    def get_history(self, session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n        key = f\"session:{session_id}:history\"\n        data = self.redis_client.get(key)\n        if data:\n            try:\n                return json.loads(data)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                return []\n        return []\n\n    def update_history(self, session_id: str, new_message: Dict[str, str]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:\n        key = f\"session:{session_id}:history\"\n        history = self.get_history(session_id)\n        \n        # Append new message\n        history.append(new_message)\n        \n        # Calculate initial total tokens\n        current_tokens = sum(self._count_message_tokens(msg) for msg in history)\n        max_tokens = settings.MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS\n        \n        # Determine minimum messages to keep (preserve system prompt if present)\n        min_messages = 0\n        if len(history) > 0 and history[0].get('role') == 'system':\n            min_messages = 1\n            \n        # Sliding window: Remove oldest non-system messages until under limit\n        while current_tokens > max_tokens and len(history) > min_messages:\n            # Identify index to remove (skip index 0 if it's system)\n            remove_idx = 0\n            if len(history) > 1 and history[0].get('role') == 'system':\n                remove_idx = 1\n            \n            removed_msg = history.pop(remove_idx)\n            # Decrement token count by the removed message's cost\n            current_tokens -= self._count_message_tokens(removed_msg)\n            \n        # Save back to Redis with TTL (30 mins)\n        self.redis_client.setex(key, 1800, json.dumps(history))\n        return history",
